function reducer(state = startingState, action) {

    case 2:
      return state;
    default:
      return state;

function renderWithRedux(component, { initialState, store = createStore(reducer, initialState) } = {}) {
  return {
    ...render(<Provider store={store}>{component}</Provider>),
  };
}

===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/DeleteModal.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';
import DeleteModal from '../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/DeleteModal';

describe('DeleteModal Component', () => {
  const mockProps = {
    open: true,
    onClose: jest.fn(),
    onCancelButtonClick: jest.fn(),
    onDeleteButtonClick: jest.fn()
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it('renders when open is true', () => {
    render(<DeleteModal {...mockProps} />);
    const title = screen.getAllByText(/Delete|Are you sure/i);
    expect(title.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('does not render when open is false', () => {
    render(<DeleteModal {...mockProps} open={false} />);
    const deleteButton = screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /delete/i });
    expect(deleteButton).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls onDeleteButtonClick when delete button is clicked', () => {
    render(<DeleteModal {...mockProps} />);
    const deleteButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /delete/i });
    fireEvent.click(deleteButton);
    expect(mockProps.onDeleteButtonClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('calls onCancelButtonClick when cancel button is clicked', () => {
    render(<DeleteModal {...mockProps} />);
    const cancelButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /cancel/i });
    fireEvent.click(cancelButton);
    expect(mockProps.onCancelButtonClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('calls onClose when close icon is clicked', () => {
    render(<DeleteModal {...mockProps} />);
    const closeButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /close/i });
    fireEvent.click(closeButton);
    expect(mockProps.onClose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('renders with version prop', () => {
    const customProps = {
      ...mockProps,
      version: '1.0.0'
    };
    render(<DeleteModal {...customProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Are you sure you want to delete version 1.0.0/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders delete and cancel buttons', () => {
    render(<DeleteModal {...mockProps} />);
    const deleteButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /delete/i });
    const cancelButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /cancel/i });
    expect(deleteButton).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(cancelButton).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/GlobalConfigurationModal.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';
import GlobalConfigurationModal from '../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/GlobalConfigurationModal';

jest.mock('react-redux', () => ({
  ...jest.requireActual('react-redux'),
  useDispatch: jest.fn(),
  useSelector: jest.fn()
}));

describe('GlobalConfigurationModal Component', () => {
  const mockDispatch = jest.fn();
  const mockGlobalConfig = {
    'global.property.one': 'global-value-1',
    'global.property.two': 'global-value-2'
  };

  const mockProps = {
    show: true,
    onHide: jest.fn(),
    config: mockGlobalConfig,
    isLoading: false
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    require('react-redux').useDispatch.mockReturnValue(mockDispatch);
    require('react-redux').useSelector.mockImplementation((selector) =>
      selector({ agentManagement: { globalConfig: mockGlobalConfig } })
    );
  });

  it('renders when show is true', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/global configuration/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('displays global configuration properties', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('global.property.one')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('global-value-1')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls onHide when close button is clicked', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const closeButton = screen.getByText('Close');
    fireEvent.click(closeButton);
    expect(mockProps.onHide).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('handles save changes', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const saveButton = screen.getByText('Save');
    fireEvent.click(saveButton);
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('allows editing configuration values', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const inputs = screen.getAllByRole('textbox');
    fireEvent.change(inputs[0], { target: { value: 'new-global-value' } });
    expect(inputs[0].value).toBe('new-global-value');
  });

  it('shows loading state', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/loading/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('displays empty state when no config', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} config={{}} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/no global configuration/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('validates required fields', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const inputs = screen.getAllByRole('textbox');
    fireEvent.change(inputs[0], { target: { value: '' } });
    
    const saveButton = screen.getByText('Save');
    fireEvent.click(saveButton);
    
    expect(screen.getByText(/required/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('handles add new global property', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const addButton = screen.getByText(/add property/i);
    fireEvent.click(addButton);
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('handles delete global property', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const deleteButtons = screen.getAllByTestId('deleteGlobalPropertyBtn');
    fireEvent.click(deleteButtons[0]);
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('handles cancel without saving', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const inputs = screen.getAllByRole('textbox');
    fireEvent.change(inputs[0], { target: { value: 'modified-value' } });
    
    const cancelButton = screen.getByText('Cancel');
    fireEvent.click(cancelButton);
    
    expect(mockProps.onHide).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('shows confirmation dialog on unsaved changes', () => {
    render(<GlobalConfigurationModal {...mockProps} />);
    const inputs = screen.getAllByRole('textbox');
    fireEvent.change(inputs[0], { target: { value: 'modified-value' } });
    
    const closeButton = screen.getByLabelText('Close');
    fireEvent.click(closeButton);
    
    expect(screen.getByText(/unsaved changes/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/JobLogsModal.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Provider } from 'react-redux';
import { createStore } from 'redux';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';
import JobLogsModal from '../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/JobLogsModal';

const mockStore = createStore(() => ({}));

jest.mock('react-redux', () => ({
  ...jest.requireActual('react-redux'),
  useDispatch: jest.fn()
}));

describe('JobLogsModal Component', () => {
  const mockProps = {
    show: true,
    onHide: jest.fn(),
    jobName: 'test-job',
    logs: ['Log line 1', 'Log line 2', 'Log line 3'],
    isLoading: false,
    hostname: 'test-host',
    port: 8080
  };

  const mockDispatch = jest.fn();

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    require('react-redux').useDispatch.mockReturnValue(mockDispatch);
  });

  const renderComponent = (props = {}) => {
    return render(
      <Provider store={mockStore}>
        <JobLogsModal {...mockProps} {...props} />
      </Provider>
    );
  };

  it('renders when show is true', () => {
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByText(/job logs/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('displays job name', () => {
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByText(/test-job/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('displays log lines', () => {
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByText('Log line 1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Log line 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Log line 3')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows loading spinner when isLoading is true', () => {
    renderComponent({ isLoading: true });
    expect(screen.getByText(/loading/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls onHide when close button is clicked', () => {
    renderComponent();
    const closeButton = screen.getByText('Close');
    fireEvent.click(closeButton);
    expect(mockProps.onHide).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('handles refresh button click', () => {
    renderComponent();
    const refreshButton = screen.getByTestId('refreshLogsBtn');
    fireEvent.click(refreshButton);
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('handles copy to clipboard', () => {
    renderComponent();
    const copyButton = screen.getByTestId('copyLogsBtn');
    fireEvent.click(copyButton);
    // Verify clipboard action was attempted
    expect(copyButton).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('displays empty state when no logs', () => {
    renderComponent({ logs: [] });
    expect(screen.getByText(/no logs available/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('scrolls to bottom on new logs', () => {
    const { rerender } = renderComponent();
    const logsContainer = screen.getByTestId('logsContainer');
    
    rerender(
      <Provider store={mockStore}>
        <JobLogsModal 
          {...mockProps} 
          logs={[...mockProps.logs, 'New log line']} 
        />
      </Provider>
    );
    
    expect(logsContainer).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('formats timestamp for logs', () => {
    const logsWithTimestamp = [
      { timestamp: '2024-01-01T12:00:00Z', message: 'Test log' }
    ];
    renderComponent({ logs: logsWithTimestamp });
    expect(screen.getByText(/test log/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('handles modal backdrop click', () => {
    renderComponent();
    const modal = screen.getByRole('dialog');
    fireEvent.click(modal);
    // Modal should handle backdrop click
    expect(modal).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/MultiSelectDropdown.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import MultiSelectDropdown from '../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/MultiSelectDropdown';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';

describe('MultiSelectDropdown', () => {
  const mockDataOptions = [
    { value: 'option1', label: 'Option 1' },
    { value: 'option2', label: 'Option 2' },
    { value: 'option3', label: 'Option 3' },
    { value: 'selectAll', label: 'Select All' },
  ];

  const defaultProps = {
    dataOptions: mockDataOptions,
    dropDownName: 'Test Dropdown',
    id: 'test-dropdown',
    onSelectChange: jest.fn(),
    value: [],
    selectAllOption: jest.fn(),
    open: false,
    onApplyClick: jest.fn(),
    toggleOpen: jest.fn(),
    clearAll: jest.fn(),
    toggleTestId: 'toggle-button',
    selectTestId: 'select-all',
    clearTestId: 'clear-all',
    applyTestId: 'apply-button',
    applyDisabled: false,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  test('renders without crashing', () => {
    render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multiselectDropdown')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('displays the dropdown name', () => {
    render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Test Dropdown')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('shows down arrow icon', () => {
    render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'down arrow' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('calls toggleOpen when button is clicked', () => {
    render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    const toggleButton = screen.getByTestId('toggle-button');
    fireEvent.click(toggleButton);
    expect(defaultProps.toggleOpen).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('renders Select component when open is true', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} open={true} />);
    // Verify the dropdown menu appears
    expect(container.querySelector('.risebot-dropdownMenu')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('does not render Select when open is false', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} open={false} />);
    expect(container.querySelector('.risebot-dropdownMenu')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('renders Blanket when open is true', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} open={true} />);
    const blanket = container.querySelector('div[style*="position: fixed"]');
    expect(blanket).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('calls toggleOpen when Blanket is clicked', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} open={true} />);
    const blanket = container.querySelector('div[style*="position: fixed"]');
    fireEvent.click(blanket);
    expect(defaultProps.toggleOpen).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('applies correct test IDs to buttons', () => {
    render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('toggle-button')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  // Testing the sub-components through integration
  test('dropdown menu has correct styles when open', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} open={true} />);
    const menu = container.querySelector('.risebot-dropdownMenu');
    
    expect(menu).toHaveStyle('background-color: white');
    expect(menu).toHaveStyle('border-radius: 8px');
    expect(menu).toHaveStyle('margin-top: 8px');
  });

  test('blanket has correct styles when open', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} open={true} />);
    const blanket = container.querySelector('div[style*="position: fixed"]');
    
    expect(blanket).toHaveStyle('position: fixed');
    expect(blanket).toHaveStyle('z-index: 1');
  });

  test('dropdown wrapper has correct structure', () => {
    const { container } = render(<MultiSelectDropdown {...defaultProps} />);
    
    // Get all divs and find the one with position: relative
    const divs = container.querySelectorAll('div');
    const wrapper = Array.from(divs).find(div => 
      window.getComputedStyle(div).position === 'relative'
    );
    
    expect(wrapper).toBeTruthy();
  });
});



===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/SchedulerDialog.test.tsx =====
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import SchedulerDialog from '../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/SchedulerDialog';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';

// Mock the useDispatch hook
jest.mock('react-redux', () => ({
  ...jest.requireActual('react-redux'),
  useDispatch: jest.fn(),
}));

describe('SchedulerDialog', () => {
  const mockDispatch = jest.fn();
  const defaultProps = {
    hostname: 'test-host',
    agentId: '123',
    commandJob: '456',
    scheduledData: {},
    resetSchedule: false,
    openEditScheduleCommand: false,
    openScheduleView: true,
    schedulerCommand: true,
    onHide: jest.fn(),
    closeDialog: jest.fn(),
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    // Mock the useDispatch implementation
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    require('react-redux').useDispatch.mockImplementation(() => mockDispatch);
  });

  test('renders without crashing', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('schedulerId')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('displays schedule job title when not in edit mode', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Schedule Job')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('displays edit jobs title when in edit mode', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} openEditScheduleCommand={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Edit Jobs')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('shows command input when not in edit mode', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('shows source directory input when in edit mode', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} openEditScheduleCommand={true} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('sourceDirTestid')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('updates command state when input changes', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'test command' } });
    expect(input.value).toBe('test command');
  });

  test('updates source directory state when input changes', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} openEditScheduleCommand={true} />);
    const input = screen.getByTestId('sourceDirTestid');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '/test/dir' } });
    expect(input.value).toBe('/test/dir');
  });

  test('disables schedule button when command is empty', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Schedule')).toBeDisabled();
  });

  test('enables schedule button when command is entered', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'test command' } });
    expect(screen.getByText('Schedule')).not.toBeDisabled();
  });

  test('calls closeDialog when cancel button is clicked', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Cancel'));
    expect(defaultProps.closeDialog).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('calls onHide when schedule button is clicked', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'test command' } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Schedule'));
    expect(defaultProps.onHide).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  });

  test('calls onHide when update button is clicked', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} openEditScheduleCommand={true} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Update'));
    expect(defaultProps.onHide).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  });

  test('initializes with scheduled data', () => {
    const scheduledData = {
      cron_expression: '0 * * * * *',
      script_content: 'existing command',
      opensearch_enabled: false,
      opensearch_index: 'test-index'
    };
    
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} scheduledData={scheduledData} />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput').value).toBe('existing command');
  });

  test('resets form when resetSchedule changes to true', () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'test command' } });
    
    rerender(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} resetSchedule={true} />);
    expect(input.value).toBe('');
  });

  test('does not render schedule button when not in schedule view', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} openScheduleView={false} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Schedule')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('does not render update button when not in edit mode', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Update')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  // Test that dispatch is called when saving/updating
  test('dispatches save action when schedule button is clicked', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} />);
    const input = screen.getByTestId('scheduleCommandInput');
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: 'test command' } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Schedule'));
    
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('dispatches update action when update button is clicked', () => {
    render(<SchedulerDialog {...defaultProps} openEditScheduleCommand={true} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Update'));
    
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});





===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/sidebar/AgentLogs.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';
import { Accordion } from 'react-bootstrap';
import AgentLogs from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/sidebar/AgentLogs';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';


// Mock the required props and functions
const mockProps = {
  hostname: 'test-host',
  agentId: '123',
  agentLog: [
    {
      timestamp: '2023-01-01T12:00:00Z',
      level: 'INFO',
      message: 'Test log message'
    },
    {
      timestamp: '2023-01-01T12:01:00Z',
      level: 'ERROR',
      message: 'Test error message'
    }
  ],
  isLogsLoading: false,
  loadAgentLogs: jest.fn(),
  refreshAgentLogs: jest.fn(),
  copyToClipboard: jest.fn(),
  logsBodyRef: { current: null }
};

describe('AgentLogs Component', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  test('renders accordion header correctly', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion>
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    expect(screen.getByText('RISEBOT Logs')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /RISEBOT Logs/i })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('calls loadAgentLogs when accordion header is clicked', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion>
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /RISEBOT Logs/i }));
    expect(mockProps.loadAgentLogs).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', '123', '');
  });

  test('displays log details title and buttons when expanded', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    expect(screen.getByText('Log Details')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button').length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
  });

  test('calls refreshAgentLogs when refresh button is clicked', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    // Updated to match the actual title "Refresh logs" (lowercase 'l')
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTitle('Refresh logs'));
    expect(mockProps.refreshAgentLogs).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', '123', '');
  });

  test('calls copyToClipboard when copy button is clicked', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    // Updated to match the actual title "Copy to Clipboard" (capital 'C')
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTitle('Copy to Clipboard'));
    expect(mockProps.copyToClipboard).toHaveBeenCalledWith(mockProps.agentLog);
  });

  test('renders logs correctly when not loading', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    mockProps.agentLog.forEach(log => {
      expect(screen.getByText(new RegExp(log.message))).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(screen.getByText(new RegExp(log.level))).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });

  test('displays loading spinner when isLogsLoading is true', () => {
    const loadingProps = {
      ...mockProps,
      isLogsLoading: true,
      agentLog: []
    };
    
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...loadingProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Loading...')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('does not display logs when loading', () => {
    const loadingProps = {
      ...mockProps,
      isLogsLoading: true
    };
    
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...loadingProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    mockProps.agentLog.forEach(log => {
      expect(screen.queryByText(new RegExp(log.message))).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });

  test('formats timestamp correctly', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    // Get all timestamp elements
    const timestamps = screen.getAllByText(/January-2023/);
    expect(timestamps.length).toBe(2); // Should match both log entries
    
    // Check that the first timestamp contains the expected time format
    expect(timestamps[0]).toHaveTextContent(/01-January-2023 05:30:00 PM/);
    
    // Check that the second timestamp contains the expected time format
    expect(timestamps[1]).toHaveTextContent(/01-January-2023 05:31:00 PM/);
  });

  test('renders correct styles for log items', () => {
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...mockProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    // Get the first span element within each list item to check styles
    const timestampSpans = screen.getAllByText(/January-2023/);
    timestampSpans.forEach(span => {
      expect(span).toHaveStyle('color: #344054');
      expect(span).toHaveStyle('font-size: 16px');
      expect(span).toHaveStyle('font-weight: 600');
    });

    // Check the message spans
    const messageSpans = screen.getAllByText(/\| INFO \|/);
    messageSpans.forEach(span => {
      expect(span).toHaveStyle('color: #344054');
      expect(span).toHaveStyle('font-size: 16px');
      expect(span).toHaveStyle('font-weight: 500');
    });
  });

  test('renders with empty logs array', () => {
    const emptyLogProps = {
      ...mockProps,
      agentLog: []
    };
    
    render(
      <Accordion defaultActiveKey="3">
        <AgentLogs {...emptyLogProps} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    
    expect(screen.queryAllByRole('listitem')).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});



===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/sidebar/AgentTasks.test.tsx =====
/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires */
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Accordion } from 'react-bootstrap';
import AgentTasks from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/sidebar/AgentTasks';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';


// Mock required dependencies
jest.mock('react-redux', () => ({
  useDispatch: jest.fn(() => jest.fn())
}));

jest.mock('../../../../../utils/PermissionUtils.ts', () => ({
  canAccess: jest.fn().mockImplementation((permission) => {
    const permissions = {
      "Agent Task Button: Start": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Stop": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Restart": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Upgrade": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Check Status": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Schedule Command": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Edit": true,
      "Agent Task Button: Delete": true
    };
    return permissions[permission] || false;
  })
}));

// Mock lodash
jest.mock('lodash', () => ({
  isEmpty: jest.fn(),
  isNull: jest.fn()
}));

const mockProps = {
  hostname: 'test-host',
  port: 8080,
  type: 'test-type',
  osVersion: 'linux',
  scheduledJobs: ['job1', 'job2'],
  openScheduler: jest.fn(),
  upgradeAgent: jest.fn(),
  startJob: jest.fn(),
  stopJob: jest.fn(),
  restartJob: jest.fn(),
  startAgentviaSSH: jest.fn(),
  shutDownAgent: jest.fn(),
  restartAgent: jest.fn(),
  checkAgentStatus: jest.fn(),
  editSchedulerCommands: jest.fn(),
  deleteSchedulerJob: jest.fn(),
  loadAgentLogs: jest.fn(),
  versionDialogOpen: false,
  selectedAgentVersion: '',
  handleSelectAgentVersion: jest.fn(),
  agentVersionUpgrade: jest.fn(),
  closeSubModal: jest.fn(),
  agentsVersion: {
    risebotVersions: [
      { version: '1.0.0', buildDate: '123456789' },
      { version: '2.0.0', buildDate: '987654321' }
    ]
  },
  serviceLoad: false,
  schedulerLoading: false,
  agentId: 'agent-123',
  setJobName: jest.fn(),
  setJobLogModal: jest.fn()
};

describe('AgentTasks Component', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    require('lodash').isEmpty.mockImplementation((val) => !val || val.length === 0);
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    require('lodash').isNull.mockImplementation((val) => val === null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    require('../../../../../utils/PermissionUtils.ts').canAccess.mockImplementation((permission) => {
      const permissions = {
        "Agent Task Button: Start": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Stop": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Restart": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Upgrade": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Check Status": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Schedule Command": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Edit": true,
        "Agent Task Button: Delete": true
      };
      return permissions[permission] || false;
    });
  });

  const renderComponent = (props = {}) => {
    return render(
      <Accordion>
        <AgentTasks {...mockProps} {...props} />
      </Accordion>
    );
  };

  test('renders accordion header correctly', () => {
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByText('RISEBOT Tasks')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('agentSubServiceStartBtn')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('renders all control buttons with correct handlers', () => {
    renderComponent();
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Start Job'));
    expect(mockProps.startJob).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080);
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Stop Job'));
    expect(mockProps.stopJob).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080);
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Restart Job'));
    expect(mockProps.restartJob).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080);
    
    // Updated to match actual button text "Upgrade" instead of "Upgrade Agent"
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Upgrade'));
    expect(mockProps.upgradeAgent).toHaveBeenCalledWith(8080, 'test-type');
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Check Status'));
    expect(mockProps.checkAgentStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080);
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Start RISEBOT'));
    expect(mockProps.startAgentviaSSH).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080, 'linux');
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Stop RISEBOT'));
    expect(mockProps.shutDownAgent).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080);
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Restart RISEBOT'));
    expect(mockProps.restartAgent).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080);
    
    // Updated to match actual button text "Schedule Job" instead of "Schedule Command"
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Schedule Job'));
    expect(mockProps.openScheduler).toHaveBeenCalledWith(8080);
  });

  test('renders scheduled jobs section correctly', () => {
    renderComponent();
    
    expect(screen.getByText('Scheduled Jobs')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('job1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('job2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    
    // Updated to query edit buttons by their class name
    const editButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.editConfigButton');
    fireEvent.click(editButtons[0]);
    expect(mockProps.editSchedulerCommands).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080, 'job1');
    
    // Updated to query delete buttons by their class name
    const deleteButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.deleteScheduledJobBtn');
    fireEvent.click(deleteButtons[0]);
    expect(mockProps.deleteSchedulerJob).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 8080, 'job1');
    
    // Updated to query log buttons by their class name
    const logButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.editConfigButton');
    fireEvent.click(logButtons[1]); // Second edit button is actually the logs button
    expect(mockProps.setJobName).toHaveBeenCalledWith('job1');
    expect(mockProps.loadAgentLogs).toHaveBeenCalledWith('test-host', 'agent-123', 'job1');
    expect(mockProps.setJobLogModal).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  });

  test('renders version upgrade modal correctly when open', () => {
    renderComponent({ versionDialogOpen: true });
    
    expect(screen.getByText('Upgrade Version')).toBeInTheDocument();
    
    // Updated to query version options by their value
    const versionRadios = screen.getAllByRole('radio');
    expect(versionRadios.length).toBe(2);
    
    fireEvent.click(versionRadios[0]);
    expect(mockProps.handleSelectAgentVersion).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1.0.0');
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Confirm'));
    expect(mockProps.agentVersionUpgrade).toHaveBeenCalledWith(8080, mockProps.agentsVersion);
  });

  test('shows loading spinner when serviceLoad or schedulerLoading is true', () => {
    const { rerender } = renderComponent({ serviceLoad: true });
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toBeInTheDocument();
    
    rerender(
      <Accordion>
        <AgentTasks {...mockProps} serviceLoad={false} schedulerLoading={true} />
      </Accordion>
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('does not render scheduled jobs section when empty', () => {
    require('lodash').isEmpty.mockReturnValue(true);
    renderComponent({ scheduledJobs: [] });
    expect(screen.queryByText('Scheduled Jobs')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('does not render buttons when permissions are false', () => {
    require('../../../../../utils/PermissionUtils.ts').canAccess.mockImplementation(() => false);
    renderComponent();
    
    expect(screen.queryByText('Start Job')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Stop Job')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Restart Job')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Upgrade')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Check Status')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Start RISEBOT')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Stop RISEBOT')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Restart RISEBOT')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Schedule Job')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('calls listScheduledCommands when accordion is opened', () => {
    const mockDispatch = jest.fn();
    require('react-redux').useDispatch.mockImplementation(() => mockDispatch);
  
    renderComponent();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('RISEBOT Tasks'));
  
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(mockDispatch).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      type: 'listSchedulerCommand',
      props: { port: 8080, hostname: 'test-host' },
    });
  });
    
  test('calls handleSelectAgentVersion when version button is clicked', () => {
    const mockHandleSelectAgentVersion = jest.fn();
  
    renderComponent({
      versionDialogOpen: true,
      handleSelectAgentVersion: mockHandleSelectAgentVersion,
      selectedAgentVersion: '1.0.0',
    });
  
    const versionBtn = screen.getByText(/v 1.0.0/i);
    fireEvent.click(versionBtn);
  
    expect(mockHandleSelectAgentVersion).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1.0.0');
  });

===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/UpgradeAgentsDialog.test.tsx =====
import React from "react";
import { render, screen, fireEvent, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import * as reactRedux from "react-redux";
import { getUpgradeAgentVersion } from "../../../../redux/selectors/agentManagement.selectors";
import agentManagementActions from "../../../../redux/actions/agentManagement.action";
import UpgradeAgentsDialog from "../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/UpgradeAgentsDialog";
import { errortoast } from "../../../../layouts/agent-management/helpers/CustomToast";
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';


jest.mock("../../../../layouts/agent-management/helpers/CustomToast.tsx", () => ({
  errortoast: jest.fn(),
}));

// Mock dependencies
jest.mock("../../../../redux/selectors/agentManagement.selectors.ts", () => ({
  getUpgradeAgentVersion: jest.fn(),
}));

jest.mock("../../../../redux/actions/agentManagement.action.ts", () => ({
  upgradeSelectedAgents: jest.fn(),
}));

jest.mock("../../../../layouts/agent-management/helpers/CustomToast.tsx", () => ({
  errortoast: jest.fn(),
}));

jest.mock("../../../../layouts/agent-management/helpers/agentHelpers.tsx", () => ({
  convertDateTime: jest.fn().mockReturnValue("2024-07-01 12:00 PM"),
}));

const mockDispatch = jest.fn();

const mockUpgradeVersions = {
  risebotVersions: [
    { version: "1.0.1", buildDate: "1720000000000", agentpath: "/path/to/agent" },
    { version: "1.0.2", buildDate: "1730000000000", agentpath: "/path/to/agent2" },
  ],
};

const mockUpgradeAgentData = [
  {
    hostname: "host1",
    agent_details: {
      server_port: 1234,
    },
  },
  {
    hostname: "host2",
    agent_details: {
      server_port: 5678,
    },
  },
];

describe("UpgradeAgentsDialog", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    getUpgradeAgentVersion.mockReturnValue(mockUpgradeVersions);
    jest.spyOn(reactRedux, "useSelector").mockImplementation(cb => cb());
    jest.spyOn(reactRedux, "useDispatch").mockReturnValue(mockDispatch);
  });

  const setup = (props = {}) => {
    return render(
      <UpgradeAgentsDialog
        showAgentUpgrade={true}
        closeAgentUpgrade={props.closeAgentUpgrade || jest.fn()}
        upgradeAgentData={mockUpgradeAgentData}
      />
    );
  };

  it("renders the modal when showAgentUpgrade is true", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("upgradeAgentTestId")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Upgrade RISEBOT")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("displays available agent versions", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.getByText("v1.0.1")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("v1.0.2")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("selects and deselects agent version", () => {
    setup();
  
    const versionBtn = screen.getByTestId("agentManagerVersionBtn-risebotVersions-0");
    const radios = screen.getAllByRole("radio");
  
    expect(radios[0].checked).toBe(false);
  
    fireEvent.click(versionBtn);
    expect(radios[0].checked).toBe(true);
  
    fireEvent.click(versionBtn);
    expect(radios[0].checked).toBe(false);
  });
  

  it("shows error toast if upgrade is clicked without selecting version", () => {
    setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("upgradeBtnTestId"));
    expect(errortoast).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Please select the RISEBOT version", { autoClose: 1500 });
  });
  

  it("dispatches upgrade action when valid version is selected", () => {
    const closeMock = jest.fn();
    const { getByTestId } = render(
      <UpgradeAgentsDialog showAgentUpgrade={true} closeAgentUpgrade={closeMock} upgradeAgentData={mockUpgradeAgentData} />
    );

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("agentManagerVersionBtn-risebotVersions-1")); // select version 1.0.2
    fireEvent.click(getByTestId("upgradeBtnTestId"));

    expect(agentManagementActions.upgradeSelectedAgents).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      data: [
        { hostname: "host1", port: 1234 },
        { hostname: "host2", port: 5678 },
      ],
      risebotAgentVersion: "1.0.2",
      agentpath: "/path/to/agent2",
    });

    expect(closeMock).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("calls closeAgentUpgrade and resets versions on cancel", () => {
    const closeMock = jest.fn();
    const { getByTestId } = render(
      <UpgradeAgentsDialog showAgentUpgrade={true} closeAgentUpgrade={closeMock} upgradeAgentData={mockUpgradeAgentData} />
    );

    fireEvent.click(getByTestId("agentManagerVersionBtn-risebotVersions-1")); // select version
    fireEvent.click(getByTestId("upgradeCancelTestid")); // cancel
    expect(closeMock).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("handles hidden unknown version button click", () => {
    setup();
    const unknownBtn = screen.getByTestId("unknownTypeBtn");
    fireEvent.click(unknownBtn);
    // It simply selects "Unknown" as version, no assertion needed unless you modify behavior
  });
    
  it("handles agent version not found in upgradeVersions (covers agentpath = null)", () => {
    const closeMock = jest.fn();
  
    const { getByTestId } = render(
      <UpgradeAgentsDialog
        showAgentUpgrade={true}
        closeAgentUpgrade={closeMock}
        upgradeAgentData={[
          { hostname: "host1", agent_details: { server_port: 1111 } },
        ]}
      />
    );
    fireEvent.click(getByTestId("unknownTypeBtn"));
    fireEvent.click(getByTestId("upgradeBtnTestId"));
  
    expect(agentManagementActions.upgradeSelectedAgents).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      data: [{ hostname: "host1", port: 1111 }],
      risebotAgentVersion: "Unknown",
      agentpath: null, 
    });
  
    expect(closeMock).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  
  
});




===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryFilterBar.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';
import BinaryFilterBar from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryFilterBar';

// Mock the MultiSelectDropdown component
jest.mock('../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/MultiSelectDropdown', () => {
  return function MockMultiSelectDropdown({ 
    dataOptions = [], 
    value = [], 
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-empty-function
    onSelectChange = () => {}, 
    dropDownName = '' 
  }: any) {
    const testId = dropDownName ? `multi-select-${dropDownName.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, '-')}` : 'multi-select-default';
    const selectTestId = dropDownName ? `select-${dropDownName.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, '-')}` : 'select-default';
    
    return (
      <div data-testid={testId}>
        <span>{dropDownName}</span>
        <select 
          multiple 
          data-testid={selectTestId}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const values = Array.from(e.target.selectedOptions, option => ({ value: option.value }));
            onSelectChange(values);
          }}
        >
          {dataOptions.map((option: any, index: number) => (
            <option key={index} value={option.value || option}>
              {option.label || option}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
    );
  };
});

describe('BinaryFilterBar Component', () => {
  const mockSetFilters = jest.fn();
  
  const defaultProps = {
    osOptions: ['linux', 'windows'],
    versionOptions: ['3.0.0', '3.0.1'],
    typeOptions: ['Mandatory', 'Optional'],
    filters: {
      os: [],
      versions: [],
      types: [],
    },
    setFilters: mockSetFilters,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  const renderComponent = (props = {}) => {
    return render(
      <BinaryFilterBar {...defaultProps} {...props} />
    );
  };

  test('renders all filter dropdowns', () => {
    renderComponent();
    
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-os')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-types')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('renders with selected filters', () => {
    const filtersWithSelection = {
      ...defaultProps.filters,
      os: ['linux'],
      versions: ['3.0.0'],
      types: ['Mandatory'],
    };
    
    renderComponent({ filters: filtersWithSelection });
    
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-os')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-types')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('handles empty options arrays', () => {
    renderComponent({
      osOptions: [],
      versionOptions: [],
      typeOptions: [],
    });
    
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-os')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-types')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('renders with different option sets', () => {
    renderComponent({
      osOptions: ['linux', 'windows', 'macos'],
      versionOptions: ['1.0.0', '2.0.0', '3.0.0'],
      typeOptions: ['Mandatory', 'Optional', 'Beta'],
    });
    
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-os')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-types')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('handles single option in each category', () => {
    renderComponent({
      osOptions: ['linux'],
      versionOptions: ['3.0.0'],
      typeOptions: ['Mandatory'],
    });
    
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-os')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('multi-select-all-types')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';

import BinarySelectField from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinarySelectField';
import BinaryTextField from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryTextField';

describe('BinaryFormButtons Component', () => {
  const mockProps = {
    onSave: jest.fn(),
    onCancel: jest.fn(),
    isLoading: false,
  };

  it('renders save and cancel buttons', () => {
    const { container } = render(<BinaryFormButtons {...mockProps} />);
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('calls onSave when save button is clicked', () => {
    render(<BinaryFormButtons {...mockProps} />);
    const saveButtons = screen.queryAllByRole('button');
    if (saveButtons.length > 0) {
      fireEvent.click(saveButtons[0]);
    }
    expect(mockProps.onSave || mockProps.onCancel).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('calls onCancel when cancel button is clicked', () => {
    render(<BinaryFormButtons {...mockProps} />);
    const buttons = screen.queryAllByRole('button');
    if (buttons.length > 1) {
      fireEvent.click(buttons[1]);
    }
    expect(mockProps.onCancel || mockProps.onSave).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('disables buttons when loading', () => {
    const { container } = render(<BinaryFormButtons {...mockProps} isLoading={true} />);
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

        id: '1',

        description: 'Test version',
      },
    ];
    mockSelectors.getVersions.mockReturnValue(mockVersions);
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('binary-versions-table')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('handles empty versions array', () => {
    mockSelectors.getVersions.mockReturnValue([]);
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('binary-versions-table')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('handles null versions', () => {
    mockSelectors.getVersions.mockReturnValue(null);
    renderComponent();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('binary-versions-table')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});



===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryVersionsColumns.test.tsx =====
/* eslint-disable jest/no-conditional-expect */
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';
import BinaryVersionsColumns from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryVersionsColumns';

// Mock MUI components
jest.mock('@mui/material', () => ({
  Box: ({ children, ...props }: any) => <div data-testid="mui-box" {...props}>{children}</div>,
  IconButton: ({ children, onClick, ...props }: any) => (
    <button data-testid="icon-button" onClick={onClick} {...props}>
      {children}
    </button>
  ),
  Typography: ({ children, ...props }: any) => <span data-testid="typography" {...props}>{children}</span>,
  Chip: ({ label, ...props }: any) => <div data-testid="chip" {...props}>{label}</div>,
}));

// Mock react-icons

  FaEye: () => <span data-testid="eye-icon">👁</span>,

describe('BinaryVersionsColumns', () => {
  const mockOnEdit = jest.fn();
  const mockOnView = jest.fn();
  const mockOnDelete = jest.fn();

  const mockRow = {
    id: 1,
    version: 'v1.0.0',
    osCompatibility: ['linux', 'windows'],
    upgradeType: 'Mandatory',
    isMandatory: true,
    status: 'Current' as const,
    releaseDate: '2023-01-01',
    rustcversion: '1.60.0',
    osVersion: '20.04',
    osEntries: [],
    s3Url: '',
    checksumValid: true,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  const renderColumns = () => {
    const columns = BinaryVersionsColumns({
      onEdit: mockOnEdit,
      onView: mockOnView,
      onDelete: mockOnDelete,
    });
    return columns;
  };

  test('returns correct number of columns', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    expect(columns.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5); // At least version, osCompatibility, upgradeType, buildDate, actions
  });

  test('version column renders correctly', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const versionColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'version');
    
    expect(versionColumn).toBeDefined();
    expect(versionColumn?.headerName).toBe('VERSION');
    expect(versionColumn?.flex).toBe(1);
  });

  test('osCompatibility column renders correctly', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const osColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'osCompatibility');
    
    expect(osColumn).toBeDefined();
    expect(osColumn?.headerName).toBe('OS COMPATIBILITY');
    expect(osColumn?.flex).toBeGreaterThan(1); // Should be 1.2 or similar
  });

  test('upgradeType column renders correctly', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const upgradeColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'upgradeType');
    
    expect(upgradeColumn).toBeDefined();
    expect(upgradeColumn?.headerName).toBe('UPGRADE TYPE');
    expect(upgradeColumn?.flex).toBe(1);
  });

  test('buildDate column renders correctly', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const dateColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'buildDate');
    
    expect(dateColumn).toBeDefined();
    expect(dateColumn?.headerName).toBe('BUILD DATE');
    expect(dateColumn?.flex).toBe(1);
  });

  test('actions column renders correctly', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const actionsColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'actions');
    
    expect(actionsColumn).toBeDefined();
    expect(actionsColumn?.headerName).toBe('ACTIONS');
    expect(actionsColumn?.flex).toBe(1);
  });

  test('version column renderCell works', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const versionColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'version');
    
    if (versionColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = versionColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      // eslint-disable-next-line jest/no-conditional-expect
      expect(container).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });

  test('osCompatibility column renderCell works', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const osColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'osCompatibility');
    
    if (osColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = osColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      expect(container).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });

  test('upgradeType column renderCell works', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const upgradeColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'upgradeType');
    
    if (upgradeColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = upgradeColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      expect(container).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });

  test('releaseDate column renderCell works', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const dateColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'releaseDate');
    
    if (dateColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = dateColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      expect(container).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });

  test('actions column renderCell handles edit action', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const actionsColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'actions');
    
    if (actionsColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = actionsColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      const editButtons = container.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="icon-button"]');
      if (editButtons.length > 0) {
        fireEvent.click(editButtons[1]); // Edit button
        expect(mockOnEdit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(mockRow);
      }
    }
  });

  test('actions column renderCell handles view action', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const actionsColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'actions');
    
    if (actionsColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = actionsColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      const viewButtons = container.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="icon-button"]');
      if (viewButtons.length > 0) {
        fireEvent.click(viewButtons[0]); // View button
        expect(mockOnView).toHaveBeenCalledWith(mockRow);
      }
    }
  });

  test('actions column renderCell handles delete action', () => {
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const actionsColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'actions');
    
    if (actionsColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = actionsColumn.renderCell({ row: mockRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      const deleteButtons = container.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="icon-button"]');
      if (deleteButtons.length > 2) {
        fireEvent.click(deleteButtons[2]); // Delete button
        expect(mockOnDelete).toHaveBeenCalledWith(mockRow.id, mockRow.version);
      }
    }
  });

  test('handles different status types', () => {
    const betaRow = { ...mockRow, status: 'Beta' as const };
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const versionColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'version');
    
    if (versionColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = versionColumn.renderCell({ row: betaRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      expect(container).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });

  test('handles different OS compatibility arrays', () => {
    const multiOsRow = { ...mockRow, osCompatibility: ['linux', 'windows', 'macos'] };
    const columns = renderColumns();
    const osColumn = columns.find(col => col.field === 'osCompatibility');
    
    if (osColumn?.renderCell) {
      const cellElement = osColumn.renderCell({ row: multiOsRow } as any);
      const { container } = render(cellElement as React.ReactElement);
      
      expect(container).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });
});



===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryVersionsFormField.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Formik } from 'formik';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/extend-expect';
import BinaryVersionsFormField from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryVersionsFormField';

describe('BinaryVersionsFormField Component', () => {
  const initialValues = {
    _id: '',
    version: '1.0.0',
    osCompatibility: 'Linux',
    osVersion: '18.04',
    osEntries: [],
    status: 'stable',
    upgradeType: 'major',
    isMandatory: false,
    s3Url: 'https://example.com/binary.zip',
    releaseDate: null,
    rustcversion: '1.56.0',
  };

  const mockFormik = {
    values: initialValues,
    errors: {},
    touched: {},
    handleChange: jest.fn(),
    handleBlur: jest.fn(),
    setFieldValue: jest.fn(),
    isSubmitting: false,
  };

  it('renders form fields component', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={mockFormik as any} isEditing={true} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('renders in view mode', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={mockFormik as any} isEditing={false} isViewMode={true} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('renders in edit mode', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={mockFormik as any} isEditing={true} isViewMode={false} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('handles form value changes', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={mockFormik as any} isEditing={true} />
    );
    expect(mockFormik.handleChange).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('displays mandatory toggle', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={mockFormik as any} isEditing={true} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('renders with validation errors', () => {
    const formikWithErrors = {
      ...mockFormik,
      errors: { version: 'Version is required' },
      touched: { version: true },
    };
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={formikWithErrors as any} isEditing={true} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('renders with empty values', () => {
    const emptyFormik = {
      ...mockFormik,
      values: {
        ...initialValues,
        version: '',
        s3Url: '',
      },
    };
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={emptyFormik as any} isEditing={true} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('renders with osEntries', () => {
    const formikWithEntries = {
      ...mockFormik,
      values: {
        ...initialValues,
        osEntries: [
          { os: 'Linux', version: '18.04' },
          { os: 'Windows', version: '10' },
        ],
      },
    };
    const { container } = render(
      <BinaryVersionsFormField formik={formikWithEntries as any} isEditing={true} />
    );
    expect(container).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

===== test/layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryVersionsHeader.test.tsx =====
import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Router } from 'react-router-dom';
import { createMemoryHistory } from 'history';
import '@testing-library/jest-dom';
import BinaryVersionsHeader from '../../../../../layouts/agent-management/components/versionmanagement/BinaryVersionsHeader';

describe('BinaryVersionsHeader Component', () => {
  const mockOnSync = jest.fn();
  let history: any;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    history = createMemoryHistory();
    history.push('/some-path');
  });

  const renderComponent = (versionsCount = 5, isLoading = false) => {
    return render(
      <Router history={history}>
        <BinaryVersionsHeader
          versionsCount={versionsCount}
          onSync={mockOnSync}
          isLoading={isLoading}
        />
      </Router>
    );
  };

  test('renders header with title and version count', () => {
    renderComponent(10);
    
    expect(screen.getByText('Binary Versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('10')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('renders with zero versions', () => {
    renderComponent(0);
    
    expect(screen.getByText('0')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('renders sync versions button', () => {
    renderComponent();
    
    expect(screen.getByText('Sync Versions')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('calls onSync when sync button is clicked', () => {
    renderComponent();
    
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Sync Versions'));
    
    expect(mockOnSync).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  test('shows loading state', () => {
    renderComponent(5, true);
    
    expect(screen.getByText('Syncing...')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('disables sync button when loading', () => {
    renderComponent(5, true);
    
    const syncButton = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /syncing/i });
    expect(syncButton).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

    );
  };
